   130	  surfaces ship in their own chunks; M2b's job is to land the
   131	  scaffolding that those chunks plug into.
   132	- It does not introduce new tokens. The full M2a token set (83
   133	  tokens post-M2a; this prototype touches none of them) is reused
   134	  verbatim. If a developer finds themselves reaching for a new
   135	  token at implementation time, they should pause and reuse before
   136	  inventing — see §7 for the discussion.
   137	
   138	## 3. Component anatomy
   139	
   140	### 3.1 Tabs primitive (`apps/frontend/src/components/Tabs.tsx`)
   141	
   142	```text
   143	<div class="tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="…" data-active-tab="metadata">
   144	  <button id="tab-transcript" role="tab" aria-controls="panel-transcript"
   145	          aria-selected="false" tabindex="-1">Transcript</button>
   146	  <button id="tab-skim"       role="tab" aria-controls="panel-skim"
   147	          aria-selected="false" tabindex="-1">Skim</button>
   148	  <button id="tab-raw"        role="tab" aria-controls="panel-raw"
   149	          aria-selected="false" tabindex="-1">Raw</button>
   150	  <button id="tab-metadata"   role="tab" aria-controls="panel-metadata"
   151	          aria-selected="true"  tabindex="0">Metadata</button>
   152	  <span class="indicator" aria-hidden="true"></span>
   153	</div>
   154	```
   155	
   156	- **`data-active-tab`** mirrors `aria-selected` for CSS hooks. The
   157	  indicator's transform is computed from the active tab's bounding
   158	  box (the simplest implementation); a future refactor can drive it
   159	  via a CSS `:has()` selector keyed off `data-active-tab` if we want
   160	  to remove the JavaScript measurement.
   161	- **`tabindex` discipline**: exactly one tab carries `tabindex="0"`
   162	  at any time (the active one); the other three carry `tabindex="-1"`.
   163	  This is the WAI-ARIA APG roving tabindex pattern. Tab from the
   164	  list pane lands on the active tab; ArrowLeft/ArrowRight cycle
   165	  through tabs; Tab from a focused tab moves to the active panel
   166	  content (NOT to the next tab).
   167	- **Indicator element**: a single `<span class="indicator">` inside
   168	  the tablist, position:absolute, bottom:-1px, **width:1px**,
   169	  height:1px, background `var(--color-accent)`. The indicator's CSS
   170	  base size is **1×1 px**; the JS sets `transform: translateX(${x}px)
   171	  scaleX(${width}px)` to grow the 1 px base extent to the active tab's
   172	  measured width. Both transforms are written into a single
   173	  `transform` declaration (one property, one transition); the
   174	  transition only animates `transform` (compositor-cheap; no layout
   175	  reflow). **Do not omit `width: 1px`** — an empty absolutely-
   176	  positioned span resolves to `width: auto` (= 0); `scaleX(N) × 0 = 0`
   177	  would render an invisible indicator.
   178	- **Disabled state pinned for future**: `aria-disabled="true"` on a
   179	  tab makes it `opacity: 0.4; cursor: not-allowed; pointer-events:
   180	  none;`. M2b ships no disabled tabs but the recipe is on file.
   181	
   182	### 3.2 SessionView shell (`apps/frontend/src/features/sessions/SessionView.tsx`)
   183	
   184	```text
   185	<article class="session-pane" key={selectedRowKey} aria-label="Session view">
   186	  {/* Narrow-mode "Back to list" quiet text-button row — only renders below 900 px. */}
   187	  <div class="back-to-list">…</div>
   188	
   189	  <header class="session-header">
   190	    <h2 class="session-title">{row.title || "(untitled)"}</h2>
   191	    <span class="tool-badge">{row.tool}</span>
   192	    <span class="badge {variant}">{row.status}</span>
   193	    {row.statusConflict && <span class="badge conflict">Conflict</span>}
   194	  </header>
   195	
   196	  <Tabs activeTab={activeTab} onActivate={setActiveTab}
   197	        defaultTab={DEFAULT_TAB_ON_SELECTION}
   198	        labels={[…Transcript / Skim / Raw / Metadata]} />
   199	
   200	  <div class="tabpanels">
   201	    <TabPanel id="panel-transcript" labelledBy="tab-transcript"
   202	              isActive={activeTab === "transcript"}
   203	              isVisited={visitedTabs.has("transcript")}>
   204	      <div key="transcript" class="tabpanel-content">…placeholder…</div>
   205	    </TabPanel>
   206	    {/* …Skim, Raw, Metadata identical structure */}
   207	  </div>
   208	</article>
   209	```
   210	
   211	- **`key={selectedRowKey}`** on the outer `<article>` — every
   212	  selection change remounts the whole subtree, which fires the
   213	  page-turn fade @keyframes.
   214	- **`activeTab` state lives on `SessionView`** (component-local,
   215	  NOT URL-synced — Resolved Decision #2). Reset to
   216	  `DEFAULT_TAB_ON_SELECTION` on `selectedRowKey` change via the same
   217	  remount.
   218	- **`visitedTabs: Set<TabId>`** state lives on `SessionView`,
   219	  initialized to `new Set([DEFAULT_TAB_ON_SELECTION])` on every
   220	  remount (= every selection change). Setting `setActiveTab(id)`
   221	  also adds `id` to `visitedTabs`.
   222	- **TabPanel** is a thin wrapper: renders the outer `<div
   223	  role="tabpanel" hidden={!isActive}>` only when `isVisited`; renders
   224	  `null` when `!isVisited`. The inner `<div key={activeTab}>` is the
   225	  cross-fade trigger — re-mounting it fires the keyframe.
   226	
   227	- **M1a → M2b minimal header delta** (verified by reading
   228	  `apps/frontend/src/features/sessions/SessionView.tsx` post-M1a +
   229	  `SessionView.test.tsx`). M1a's `ready-placeholder` state ships
   230	  ONLY a single line of muted prose ("Session view coming in
   360	- **Focus-visible**: 2 px outline of `--color-accent`, outline-offset
   361	  2 px, border-radius 2 px (matches M1a focus-visible recipe).
   362	- **Pressed (mouse-down)**: snaps to `--color-ink` without
   363	  transitioning the color (the tab is about to become active anyway).
   364	- **Disabled (pinned for future use)**: `aria-disabled="true"` →
   365	  `opacity: 0.4`, `cursor: not-allowed`, `pointer-events: none`.
   366	
   367	### 4.3 Active indicator slide direction
   368	- **Left → right** (tab N → N+M, M > 0): indicator slides positively
   369	  along `translateX`, indicator width animates to the new tab's text
   370	  width via `scaleX`.
   371	- **Right → left** (tab N → N–M): same mechanism; transform is purely
   372	  compositor work so direction doesn't matter for performance.
   373	- Initial mount: indicator settles on the default tab via a
   374	  `requestAnimationFrame` callback (Fraunces swap can shift metrics
   375	  by ~1 px per M2a §4 fallback math; the rAF defers measurement by
   376	  one frame so the indicator anchors correctly).
   377	
   378	### 4.4 Tab panel mounting matrix
   379	
   380	| State                    | DOM         | `hidden` | a11y tree | content rendered |
   381	|--------------------------|-------------|----------|-----------|------------------|
   382	| not-yet-visited          | not present | n/a      | n/a       | no               |
   383	| visited-but-inactive     | present     | true     | excluded  | yes (cached)     |
   384	| active                   | present     | false    | included  | yes              |
   385	
   386	On `selectedRowKey` change: `visitedTabs` resets, all four panels
   387	unmount cleanly. Cache (in `useParsedSession`, M3) persists across
   388	selection changes so re-selecting the same session re-mounts a panel
   389	that finds a cache hit — but the M2b panels (Metadata, Raw) don't
   390	use that cache anyway (Metadata reads SessionRow only; Raw uses its
   391	own `consumeRawPreview` consumer).
   392	
   393	### 4.5 Tab panel cross-fade direction
   394	
   395	Cross-fade is **fade-IN-only** (per spec line 87 + planner refinement):
   396	
   397	- Outgoing panel: hidden via `hidden` HTML attribute → `display: none`
   398	  immediately. NO fade-out animation.
   399	- Incoming panel: inner `<div key={activeTab}>` re-mounts → CSS
   400	  `@keyframes tab-cross-fade` fires (opacity 0 → 1 over 120 ms
   401	  `--ease-out`).
   402	
   403	The asymmetry is intentional: a true cross-fade (out + in
   404	overlapping) would require two panels rendered simultaneously, which
   405	fights both the `hidden` attribute (display:none can't transition)
   406	AND the lazy-mount discipline. Fade-in-only matches spec intent and
   407	keeps the panel mounting story simple.
   408	
   409	### 4.6 Page-turn fade direction
   410	
   411	Page-turn fade is **entrance only**:
   412	
   413	- Previous selection: removed entirely via React key change (the
   414	  previous `<SessionView>` unmounts).
   415	- Incoming SessionView: opacity 0 → 1 + translateX(4px → 0) over
   416	  200 ms `--ease-out`. The transform direction is consistent (always
   417	  in from the right by 4 px) — this is signature detail #2's
   418	  "opening a folder" gesture, not a directional history scrub.
   419	- **Clicking the already-selected row is a React no-op**:
   420	  `key={selectedRowKey}` is unchanged → no remount → no animation.
   421	  React's `setState` with an identical value short-circuits the
   422	  re-render at the parent that owns selection (`App.tsx` per
   423	  Resolved Decision #20), so the SessionView doesn't even receive
   424	  a new prop. The page-turn fade fires only when `selectedRowKey`
   425	  actually changes value (including null → key, key → different key,
   426	  key → null transitions).
   427	
   428	### 4.7 Reduced-motion fallbacks
   429	
   430	Single global rule in `apps/frontend/src/styles/global.css` (already
   530	   4. Active panel content: focusable elements within (Copy path
   531	      button, Open raw anchor, Retry button, prototype's state
   532	      radios).
   533	4. (No further focus targets unless the panel has its own — Skim
   534	   placeholder + Transcript placeholder have no focusable
   535	   children.)
   536	
   537	## 5. Motion & interaction
   538	
   539	### 5.1 Animatable surfaces in M2b
   540	
   541	| Surface                  | Property                | Duration              | Easing                       | Trigger                    |
   542	|--------------------------|-------------------------|-----------------------|------------------------------|----------------------------|
   543	| Tab indicator slide      | `transform: translateX` + `scaleX` | `--motion-base` (120 ms) | `--ease-standard`         | active-tab change          |
   544	| Tab indicator color (initial mount) | (none — instant) | n/a                   | n/a                          | first paint                |
   545	| Tab text color (active ↔ inactive shift) | `color`         | `--motion-fast` (80 ms) | `--ease-standard`        | active-tab change          |
   546	| Tab text color (hover)   | `color`                 | `--motion-fast` (80 ms) | `--ease-standard`           | pointer enter              |
   547	| Tab panel cross-fade-IN  | `opacity` (0 → 1)       | `--motion-base` (120 ms) | `--ease-out`              | active-tab change (re-mount of inner `key={activeTab}`) |
   548	| Page-turn fade           | `opacity` (0 → 1) + `transform: translateX` (4px → 0) | `--motion-disclosure` (200 ms) | `--ease-out` | `selectedRowKey` change (re-mount of `<SessionView key={…}>`) |
   549	| Copy path button hover   | `background-color`      | `--motion-fast` (80 ms) | `--ease-standard`           | pointer enter              |
   550	| Retry button hover       | `background-color`      | `--motion-fast` (80 ms) | `--ease-standard`           | pointer enter              |
   551	| Open raw anchor hover    | `border-color`          | `--motion-fast` (80 ms) | `--ease-standard`           | pointer enter              |
   552	
   553	All values come from existing M2a tokens; no new motion tokens added.
   554	
   555	The text-color transition on active-tab changes is intentionally
   556	tiny (80 ms vs. the 120 ms indicator slide) — the indicator
   557	provides the dominant motion signal; the text-color shift is just
   558	enough to feel responsive without competing.
   559	
   560	### 5.2 The two-layer keyed-React pattern (load-bearing for the chunk)
   561	
   562	The spec creates a tension between two requirements:
   563	- Spec line 654 mandates `hidden` for inactive-but-visited panels
   564	  (display: none + accessibility-tree exclusion).
   565	- Spec line 87 mandates a 120 ms opacity cross-fade on tab change.
   566	
   567	`display: none` cannot transition to `display: block` smoothly
   568	(prior to interpolate-size keyword support; even then, opacity
   569	doesn't transition through display changes). The resolution: the
   570	cross-fade is **fade-IN-only**, driven by a CSS `@keyframes` that
   571	fires on the inner content's mount, not on the outer panel's
   572	`hidden` toggle.
   573	
   574	```jsx
   575	<div role="tabpanel" hidden={!isActive}>
   576	  <div key={activeTab}>
   577	    {/* React unmounts + re-mounts this on every activeTab change */}
   578	    {/* The CSS @keyframes tab-cross-fade fires every mount */}
   579	  </div>
   580	</div>
   581	```
   582	
   583	Same trick applied at the outer level for the page-turn fade:
   584	
   585	```jsx
   586	<article class="session-pane" key={selectedRowKey}>
   587	  {/* React remounts this on every selectedRowKey change */}
   588	  {/* The CSS @keyframes session-page-turn fires every mount */}
   589	</article>
   590	```
   591	
   592	The two keyframes fire on **separate elements** (one is the
   593	SessionPane root, one is the inner panel content), so they never
   594	collide. When the user selects a different session AND the new
   595	selection lands on a different default tab, both fire — the
   596	SessionPane mounts with the page-turn fade (200 ms), and the
   597	default panel's content mounts with the cross-fade (120 ms). The
   598	two animations run in parallel on different DOM nodes; the
   599	compositor handles them independently.
   600	
   601	### 5.3 Reduced-motion behavior
   602	
   603	Under `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` (or the prototype's
   604	`data-motion="reduce"` toggle):
   605	
   606	- `--motion-base` and `--motion-disclosure` durations are zeroed by
   607	  the global reduced-motion rule. The keyframes still run for one
   608	  paint frame (0.01 ms), so the visual outcome is "snap to opacity
   609	  1 + translateX(0)" without any visible animation.
   610	- The indicator's `transition: transform` is also zeroed. On
   611	  active-tab change the indicator teleports.
   612	- The reading-wash noise overlay is suppressed (M2a established).
   613	
   614	## 6. Accessibility
   615	
   616	### 6.1 ARIA roles and properties
   617	
   618	Per WAI-ARIA APG tabs pattern (`https://www.w3.org/WAI/ARIA/apg/patterns/tabs/`):
   619	
   620	- `<div role="tablist" aria-label="Session content tabs">` — the tab
   930	The developer verifies each item before requesting review.
   931	
   932	### Tabs primitive (`apps/frontend/src/components/Tabs.tsx`)
   933	
   934	1. Tablist DOM is `<div role="tablist" aria-label={...}>` containing
   935	   exactly four `<button role="tab">` children plus one
   936	   `<span class="indicator" aria-hidden="true">` child.
   937	2. Each `<button role="tab">` has `id`, `aria-controls` (= panel id),
   938	   `aria-selected` (`"true"` exactly when active, otherwise `"false"`).
   939	3. Roving tabindex: exactly one tab carries `tabindex="0"` at any
   940	   moment (the active one); the other three carry `tabindex="-1"`.
   941	4. ArrowLeft on active tab → previous tab (wraps from first to last).
   942	5. ArrowRight on active tab → next tab (wraps from last to first).
   943	6. Home → first tab; End → last tab.
   944	7. Click on tab → activates it; focus moves to it.
   945	8. Tab strip uses arrow keys, NOT Tab, for between-tab navigation
   946	   (Tab moves focus out of the tablist into the active panel).
   947	9. Indicator slides between tabs over `var(--motion-base)` (120 ms)
   948	   with `var(--ease-standard)`, transitioning ONLY `transform`. No
   949	   layout-touching properties animate. Suppressed under
   950	   `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`.
   951	10. Indicator color is `var(--color-accent)`; **base CSS box is
   952	    exactly `width: 1px; height: 1px;`** (NOT `width: auto` — see
   953	    item 11 for why); positioned via `position: absolute; bottom:
   954	    -1px; left: 0;` so it sits on the strip's bottom hairline border.
   955	11. Indicator width matches the active tab's text width (NOT the
   956	    tab's full padding box). Implemented as: indicator base size is
   957	    `width: 1px; height: 1px;` (item 10), and the JS `useLayoutEffect`
   958	    writes a single `transform: translateX(${x}px) scaleX(${width}px)`
   959	    where `x = activeTabRect.left - tablistRect.left` and `width =
   960	    activeTabRect.width`. The 1 px base is load-bearing: without it,
   961	    `scaleX(N) × 0 = 0` and the indicator never appears. Both
   962	    components live in one `transform` declaration so the
   963	    `transition: transform var(--motion-base) var(--ease-standard)`
   964	    animates them together as a single compositor-cheap step.
   965	12. Active tab text: `var(--color-ink)`, `font-weight: 600`,
   966	    `var(--font-chrome)`, `var(--text-sm)`.
   967	13. Inactive tab text: `var(--color-ink-muted)`, regular weight, same
   968	    family + size.
   969	14. Hover (inactive only): text shifts to
   970	    `color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-ink) 80%, var(--color-ink-muted))`.
   971	    NO background tint.
   972	15. Focus-visible: `outline: 2px solid var(--color-accent);
   973	    outline-offset: 2px; border-radius: 2px;`.
   974	16. Tab strip has `height: 2.5rem`, `padding: 0 var(--space-5)`,
   975	    `gap: var(--space-3)` (12 px), `border-bottom: 1px solid var(--color-border)`.
   976	17. NO `background-color` on the tab strip itself; strip inherits
   977	    `var(--color-surface)`.
   978	
   979	### SessionView (`apps/frontend/src/features/sessions/SessionView.tsx`)
   980	
   981	18. `<SessionView>` is keyed on `selectedRowKey` so React remounts on
   982	    selection change.
   983	19. `.session-pane` has CSS `@keyframes session-page-turn` that runs
   984	    once on mount: `from { opacity: 0; transform: translateX(4px) }
   985	    to { opacity: 1; transform: translateX(0) }`, duration
   986	    `var(--motion-disclosure)` (200 ms), easing `var(--ease-out)`,
   987	    `animation-fill-mode: both`. The `to` state explicitly sets
   988	    `translateX(0)` (NOT `transform: none`) so the property remains
   989	    declared and does not interact poorly with later inline
   990	    `transform` writes during the same paint. Suppressed under
   991	    reduced-motion.
   992	20. `activeTab` is component-local React state, initialized to
   993	    `DEFAULT_TAB_ON_SELECTION` (= `"metadata"` at M2b).
   994	21. `visitedTabs: Set<TabId>` is component-local React state,
   995	    initialized to `new Set([DEFAULT_TAB_ON_SELECTION])`.
   996	22. `setActiveTab(id)` → updates `activeTab` AND adds `id` to
   997	    `visitedTabs` (via `setVisitedTabs(prev => new Set([...prev, id]))`).
   998	23. Both `activeTab` and `visitedTabs` reset to defaults on
   999	    `selectedRowKey` change (via the `key` remount; no manual reset
  1000	    needed).
  1001	24. The minimal header renders `<h2 class="session-title">` in
  1002	    italic Fraunces at `--text-xl`, color `var(--color-ink)` (or
  1003	    `var(--color-ink-muted)` italic when row.title is null with
  1004	    "(untitled)" fallback). Tool badge in `--font-mono --text-xs`
  1005	    with hairline border. Status pill via Phase 4 `.badge {variant}`
  1006	    recipe. Conflict badge (variant `conflict` = warn recipe) only
  1007	    when `row.statusConflict`.
  1008	25. M2b minimal header does NOT include Copy path button, header-side
  1009	    subagent badge, sourcePathIsStale hint, or "Open raw" anchor.
  1010	    Those defer to M4.
  1011	26. Narrow-viewport "← Back to list" quiet text-button row sits ABOVE
  1012	    the `.session-header` and only displays under
  1013	    `@media (max-width: 899.98px)`. Click sets
  1014	    `narrowMode = "list"` only (preserves selection + URL + tab/scroll
  1015	    state per Resolved Decision #17).
  1016	
  1017	### Tab panel mounting + cross-fade
  1018	
  1019	27. Each `<div role="tabpanel" id="panel-{id}" aria-labelledby="tab-{id}"
  1020	    hidden={!isActive}>` is rendered ONLY when `visitedTabs.has(id)`.
  1021	    Unvisited panels are NOT in the DOM. **Panel rendering MUST be
  1022	    object-keyed (`key={tabId}`), NEVER index-keyed (`key={index}`)**
  1023	    so that skipping an unvisited mid-sequence panel never causes
  1024	    React to re-key the remaining panels by position. Required
  1025	    pattern (or strictly equivalent):
  1026	
  1027	    ```jsx
  1028	    {(Object.entries({
  1029	       transcript: <TranscriptPanel />,
  1030	       skim:       <SkimPanel />,
  1031	       raw:        <RawTab row={row} />,
  1032	       metadata:   <SessionMetadata row={row} />,
  1033	     }) as Array<[TabId, ReactNode]>)
  1034	       .filter(([id]) => visitedTabs.has(id))
  1035	       .map(([id, content]) => (
  1036	         <TabPanel key={id} id={id} isActive={id === activeTab}>
  1037	           {content}
  1038	         </TabPanel>
  1039	       ))}
  1040	    ```
  1041	
  1042	    `key={id}` (the tab id string), NEVER `key={index}` from
  1043	    `.map((_, i) => …)`. Index-keying would cause the wrong panel to
  1044	    remount when a previously-unvisited mid-sequence tab is
  1045	    activated for the first time, defeating the lazy-mount
  1046	    invariant and breaking the cross-fade trigger semantics.
  1047	28. The inner `<div key={activeTab} class="tabpanel-content">` carries
  1048	    a CSS `@keyframes tab-cross-fade` that runs once on mount:
  1049	    `from { opacity: 0 } to { opacity: 1 }`, duration
  1050	    `var(--motion-base)` (120 ms), easing `var(--ease-out)`,
  1051	    `animation-fill-mode: both`. Suppressed under reduced-motion.
  1052	29. Cross-fade is fade-IN-only — outgoing panel hides instantly via
  1053	    the `hidden` attribute (display: none); no fade-out animation.
  1054

   262	/* ===== @font-face — Fraunces self-hosted (Phase 5 / M2a) =====
   263	 * Two declarations: Roman + Italic. The upstream Fraunces is shipped
   264	 * as two separate variable fonts (Roman, Italic) rather than one
   265	 * variable font with an italic axis, so each `font-style` value points
   266	 * at a different `.woff2` file. Both are subset to Latin Basic +
   267	 * Latin-1 Supplement + General Punctuation (`U+0020-007E,
   268	 * U+00A0-00FF, U+2010-2027, U+2030-2052`) with kerning, ligatures,
   269	 * small-caps, and old-style numerals. Subsetting + axis-pinning recipe
   270	 * documented at `docs/dependency-rules.md` §"Phase 5 M2a notes".
   271	 *
   272	 * `font-display: swap` — Fraunces NEVER blocks first paint. The
   273	 * fallback (Charter / Iowan Old Style / Georgia) renders immediately
   274	 * and Fraunces swaps in when the woff2 finishes loading. The
   275	 * `size-adjust` + `ascent-override` + `descent-override` values are
   276	 * tuned to Charter's metrics (the most common system serif on
   277	 * macOS / iOS) so the swap is invisible — line heights stay within
   278	 * 1 px on every step in the type scale. Designer-recommended starting
   279	 * values per design.md §"Fraunces fallback math"; revisit after the
   280	 * M6 manual font-swap measurement.
   281	 *
   282	 * Reversibility (Resolved Decision #15): deleting both @font-face
   283	 * blocks AND the two woff2 files yields a still-cohesive
   284	 * system-serif aesthetic. No font is required for the page to
   285	 * render. */
   286	/* unicode-range narrowing rationale (M2a developer): design.md item 12
   287	 * lists `U+0000-007F, U+00A0-00FF, U+2000-206F` as the subset coverage.
   288	 * The implemented ranges below tighten that:
   289	 *   - `U+0020-007E` instead of `U+0000-007F` — strips ASCII control
   290	 *     characters (NUL through US, plus DEL) which are non-printing and
   291	 *     never appear on the inspection surface. Trims the @font-face
   292	 *     glyph-table hint without affecting any visible glyph.
   293	 *   - `U+2010-2027, U+2030-2052` instead of `U+2000-206F` — keeps the
   294	 *     General Punctuation glyphs Fraunces actually renders (en/em-dash,
   295	 *     hyphenation point, primes, ellipsis, per-thousand sign,
   296	 *     letterlike-numerals slash) and drops the `U+2028-202F` line/word
   297	 *     separator + bidi-control range plus `U+2053-206F` deprecated
   298	 *     swung-dash + invisible-format range. Subset script's actual glyph
   299	 *     emission already excludes those — the unicode-range here matches
   300	 *     the subset content rather than overstating coverage.

     1	# M2a WCAG AA contrast verification
     2	
     3	This is the comprehensive contrast table for every visible
     4	foreground/background pair the M1a + M1b consumers depend on, under
     5	the new oklch ramp. Ratios are now **codex-measured** (not designer-
     6	estimated): the M2a fix-up round 1 ran a Python WCAG 2.1 contrast
     7	script that converts each oklch value via the canonical
     8	oklch → oklab → linear sRGB → sRGB → relative luminance pipeline
     9	and computes `((L1 + 0.05) / (L2 + 0.05))` for every pair. The
    10	designer's original CIE2000-anchored estimates were optimistic —
    11	codex's numerical computation is authoritative and what now lands
    12	in this artifact.
    13	
    14	## Pass / fail table
    15	
    16	WCAG AA gates: **4.5:1** for normal text (< 18 pt or < 14 pt bold),
    17	**3:1** for large text and non-text UI components (SC 1.4.11). Every
    18	text pair below is treated as normal text unless explicitly noted as
    19	"large".
    20	
    21	The values reflect the post-fix-up oklch ramp:
    22	- `--color-accent` light = `oklch(55% 0.15 50)` (was `60%` pre-fix-up;
    23	  codex measured the original at 3.93:1 vs surface — failed AA text).
    24	- `--color-border-strong` light = `oklch(65% 0.02 70)` (was `70%`;
    25	  codex measured original at 2.53:1 — failed SC 1.4.11).
    26	- `--color-border-strong` dark = `oklch(48% 0.02 70)` (was `40%`;
    27	  codex measured original at 2.13:1 — failed SC 1.4.11).

   148	  light / `oklch(72% 0.13 60)` dark); the warn pill + warn toast
   149	  border recipes still consume warn through mixes that already

   166	  tightens the rounding rule.
   167	
   168	## Table-row hover/select stack-up
   169	
   170	The M1a row state recipe stacks hover, selection, and pulse tints in
   171	predictable layers. The §6 + §7 deep-link-pulse-peak measurement
   172	(12.62:1 light / 11.83:1 dark for `--color-ink` against the pulse
   173	peak) is the WORST-CASE moment in the row's animation — the brief
   174	0% keyframe instant when the accent tint is at 22% mix. Even at this
   175	peak the body text clears AAA in both modes. **No change needed
   176	to the M1a + M1b row state recipe under M2a.**
   177	
   178	## Hairline visibility (decorative, not WCAG-gated)
   179	
   180	`--color-border` against `--color-surface` lands at 1.49:1 (light) /
   181	1.35:1 (dark). Below WCAG floors, but this is intentional — the
   182	hairline is a decorative separator, not a UI border per SC 1.4.11.
   183	The Archive-room aesthetic specifically wants "hairline over shadow"
   184	(spec line 25), and a hairline that clears 3:1 reads as a heavy
   185	divider rather than a quiet line.
   186	
   187	The sticky-bar top edge uses `--color-border-strong` (3.06:1 light /
   188	3.00:1 dark, post fix-up) — that pair MUST clear 3:1 because the
   189	sticky bar is a chrome surface separator that demarcates the action
   190	bar from the scrolling list above it.
   191	
   192	## Automated re-measurement
   193	
   194	The Phase 5 / M2a contrast script lives at `/tmp/wcag_check.py`
   195	during the fix-up round (one-off measurement tool, not committed to
   196	the repo per Resolved Decision #16's "no third runtime dep" rule).
   197	The script:
   198	1. Hardcodes the post-fix-up oklch values for every M2a token.
   199	2. Converts each oklch value to sRGB via the canonical CSS Color L4
   200	   formula (~30 lines of inline math; no `culori` dep).
   201	3. For each pair in the table above, computes the WCAG 2.1 contrast
   202	   ratio: `((L1 + 0.05) / (L2 + 0.05))`.
   203	4. Emits a Markdown-style summary.
   204	
   205	The script was run once during M2a fix-up round 1 to produce the
   206	authoritative ratios above. Re-run only if a future round changes
   207	oklch tokens.
   208	
   209	## Hex fallback contrast (`@supports not (color: oklch(0% 0 0))`)
   210	
   211	The fallback layer's `--color-border-strong` was lifted in tandem
   212	with the oklch fix to keep parity:
   213	- Light hex `#8a909c` vs `#f8f9fb` surface: **3.04:1** (passes 3:1).
   214	- Dark hex `#5e6571` vs `#151821` surface: **3.02:1** (passes 3:1).
   215

   217	1.53:1 / 1.71:1 — failing 3:1. The fallback layer therefore now
   218	clears WCAG SC 1.4.11 in addition to the oklch primary layer. The
   219	remaining 22 hex literals are unchanged (Phase 4 cool-blue
   220	aesthetic; documented regression per design.md §9.3).

  1138	## Resolved Decisions (from planner)
  1139	
  1140	These decisions are resolved; future planners/reviewers should treat them as load-bearing assumptions, not as open questions:
  1141	
  1142	1. **`replaceState` over `pushState`** for `?session=` sync (no back-stack pollution; revisit only on user feedback).
  1143	2. **`?tab=...` NOT URL-synced** in Phase 5 (deferred; tab state is component-local React state).
  1144	3. **No search-within-transcript** in Phase 5 (deferred until annotations land in a future phase).
  1145	4. **Oversized-user-message threshold = 64 KB** (constant `USER_MSG_OVERSIZE_THRESHOLD`).
  1146	5. **Full-document fetch cap = 5 MB** (constant `STREAM_RAW_TEXT_BYTE_CAP`); truncation banner directs users to the session-header **"Open raw"** anchor (which opens the full uncapped `/api/v1/sessions/<storedSessionUid>/raw` endpoint in a new tab) as the escape hatch for > 5 MB payloads. The Raw tab itself remains capped at 256 KB / 20 lines (byte-equivalent to Phase 4) and is the verifiability hatch for *small-to-medium* payloads.
  1147	6. **`Drawer.tsx` + `SessionDetail.tsx` DELETED in M6** (no future modal in Phase 5).
  1148	7. **`focus-trap-react` left installed** as orphan after drawer retirement (negligible cost; future modal needs may revive).
  1149	8. **Subagent sidecar badge appears in the session header AND the Metadata tab** (header for at-a-glance discovery, Metadata for canonical record); row stays at 4 essentials (no badge in the compact list).
  1150	9. **Skim "Expand to raw messages" reuses TranscriptView** scoped to messageIndex range (typed messages, not raw NDJSON; Raw tab is the verifiability hatch for raw bytes).
  1151	10. **Aesthetic direction = "Archive-room"** (warm-paper / deep-ink / sienna accent / hairlines / sharp corners / restrained motion). Documented in §Design Language.
  1152	11. **Default active tab progression** (revised from initial "Skim always" recommendation):
  1153	    - M2 close → default = **Metadata** (the only fully-functional parsed-content-free tab at M2; Skim + Transcript still render "Coming soon" copy).
  1154	    - M4 close → default shifts to **Transcript** (Transcript becomes functional; shows real content immediately).
  1155	    - M5 close → default stays **Transcript** (Skim becomes functional but Transcript is the better landing surface until LLM summaries land in a future phase).
  1156	    The default-tab choice is a single constant `DEFAULT_TAB_ON_SELECTION` exported from `SessionView.tsx`; shifts at M4 are a one-line edit + one test update. Skim's disabled-summary placeholders would otherwise feel broken at first glance.
  1157	12. **Tab panel mounting strategy = lazy on first activation, then keep mounted** (`visitedTabs: Set<TabId>` tracked in `SessionView` state). Inactive-but-visited panels carry the `hidden` attribute. Avoids the contradiction of mounting heavy parsed-content tabs (Skim/Transcript) before they're visited (which would prematurely trigger the 5 MB fetch). Resets on `selectedRowKey` change.
  1158	13. **`useParsedSession` cache = LRU bounded at 5 entries** keyed by `${storedSessionUid}::${tool}` (`USE_PARSED_SESSION_CACHE_MAX = 5`). Module-scoped epoch counter `cacheEpoch` is bumped on Rescan AND successful Import; in-flight fetches drop their result if the epoch changed mid-flight. Hard cache clear also fires on both Rescan and Import.
  1159	14. **Per-tool parser dispatch = registry, not switch** (`PARSERS: Record<Tool, ParserFn>` in `parsers/index.ts`). Typechecks for exhaustiveness; adding a third tool is one record entry.
  1160	15. **Display font = Fraunces (variable, self-hosted woff2 ~80 KB subsetted)** with system-serif fallback. Self-hosting a static font asset is NOT a runtime dependency under the spec's dep policy; documented in `docs/dependency-rules.md` at M2. Reversible via single @font-face deletion.
  1161	16. **Color space = `oklch()`** as source-of-truth with `@supports not (color: oklch(0% 0 0))` hex fallback layer (the existing 24 hex literals).
  1162	17. **`Esc` key vs "← Back to list" button are DISTINCT gestures**: Esc fully clears selection (calls `setSelectedRowKey(null)` + URL `?session=` clear + resets `narrowMode = "list"`); Back to list only sets `narrowMode = "list"` (preserves selection + URL + tab/scroll state, so re-tapping the row brings the user back exactly where they left off). Esc is scoped: ignored when focus is in editable controls (`<input>`, `<textarea>`, `[contenteditable]`, `role="combobox"`).
  1163	18. **Metadata + Raw tabs do NOT trigger `useParsedSession` fetch.** Selecting a session and going straight to Metadata or Raw never triggers the 5 MB full-document fetch. Only Skim/Transcript trigger it.
  1164	19. **Deep-link arrival fires the deep-link pulse animation**; click-driven selection does not. Distinguishes the two paths visually for a returning user who pasted a URL.
  1165	20. **Selection ownership in `App.tsx`** (`useSelectedSession` hook lives there). `SessionsView.tsx` is presentational — receives `selectedRowKey` + `onSelectRow` as props. Matches the existing Phase 4 pattern for `pageIndex` ownership. Closes the M1-planner question.
  1166	
  1167	## Open Considerations
  1168	
  1169	(Selection ownership resolved — see Resolved Decision #20.)

  1171	(`focus-trap-react` orphan-install resolved — see Resolved Decision #7. Listed here only as a reminder for a *future-phase* spec author who may want to revisit and uninstall to free escape-hatch slot accounting if the dep budget needs reclaiming.)
  1172	- **Fraunces subset preparation**: M2 planner picks the trade-off:
  1173	  - Option A (recommended): subset the Fraunces variable woff2 ONCE locally (using e.g. `pyftsubset` or fonttools), commit the resulting ~80 KB woff2 to `apps/frontend/public/fonts/` as a static asset. **No CI build step**, no runtime dep, no per-build subsetting cost. The subset file is regenerated only when Fraunces upstream changes (rare — Phase 5 timeline, almost never).
  1174	  - Option B: ship the full ~250 KB variable file as-downloaded. Simpler one-time setup; larger payload.
  1175	  Default: Option A. Documented in `docs/dependency-rules.md` at M2 with a one-line "regenerate via `pyftsubset` if upstream Fraunces revs". Subsetting is a one-time author-side step, NOT a build step.
  1176	- **Truth-table additions for future tool versions**: M3 planner reviews the truth-table fixture matrix against any new Claude Code or Codex output shapes since Phase 4 spec time. If new shapes exist, expand the matrix at M3 (not Phase 6+); the parsers themselves are point-in-time correct only against the documented matrix.

   620	- `<div role="tablist" aria-label="Session content tabs">` — the tab
   621	  strip container.
   622	- `<button role="tab" aria-selected={true|false} aria-controls={panelId}
   623	  tabindex={0|-1}>` — each tab. The `<button>` element handles the
   624	  Enter/Space activation natively; the explicit `aria-selected`
   625	  attribute carries the active state.
   626	- `<div role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby={tabId} hidden={!isActive}>`
   627	  — each panel. The `hidden` attribute hides the panel from the
   628	  accessibility tree AND from the layout (display: none). When
   629	  `isActive`, the panel is rendered visible.
   630	- `aria-controls` ↔ `aria-labelledby` linkage forms the tab ↔ panel
   631	  relationship.
   632	
   633	### 6.2 Keyboard interaction matrix
   634	
   635	| Key                | When focus is on…                      | Effect                          |
   636	|--------------------|----------------------------------------|---------------------------------|
   637	| Tab (forward)      | list pane content                      | focus moves to the tablist (lands on the active tab) |
   638	| Tab (forward)      | a tab in the tablist                   | focus moves OUT of the tablist into the active panel content (NOT to the next tab) |
   639	| Tab (backward)     | a tab in the tablist                   | focus moves backward to the list pane |
   640	| Tab (backward)     | active panel content                   | focus moves to the active tab in the tablist |
   641	| ArrowLeft          | a tab in the tablist                   | active tab decrements (wraps to last on first) |
   642	| ArrowRight         | a tab in the tablist                   | active tab increments (wraps to first on last) |
   643	| Home               | a tab in the tablist                   | active tab = first (Transcript) |
   644	| End                | a tab in the tablist                   | active tab = last (Metadata) |
   645	| Enter / Space      | a tab in the tablist                   | no-op — selection already followed focus per the automatic-activation pattern |
   646	| Esc                | (any focus)                            | M1a behavior — clears selection (`setSelectedRowKey(null)` + URL `?session=` clear). Esc is scoped per Resolved Decision #17 (ignored when focus is in editable controls). M2b does NOT change Esc semantics. |
   647	
   648	**Selection follows focus** (automatic activation). Per WAI-ARIA APG:
   649	"if it is easy to determine which tab will receive focus based on
   650	the keyboard event, automatic activation is appropriate." Our four
   651	tabs are all instantly cheap (Metadata / Raw never trigger the 5 MB
   652	fetch per Resolved Decision #18; Skim / Transcript at M2b are
   653	placeholders). Automatic activation is the better default.
   654	
   655	### 6.3 Focus management
   656	
   657	- On `selectedRowKey` change: the `<SessionView>` remounts; focus is
   658	  NOT automatically moved to the new pane (the user clicked a row;
   659	  focus stayed on the row). The new SessionView mounts with the
   660	  default tab pre-selected, but the tablist is not stolen-focused.
   661	- On Esc / "Back to list" / direct URL clear: focus returns to the
   662	  list pane (Phase 4 / M1a behavior; M2b does not change this).
   663	- On programmatic `activate(id)` from keyboard nav: focus is moved
   664	  to the newly-active tab via `tab.focus()`. From click: focus moves
   665	  to the clicked tab (the browser's default `<button>` behavior).
   666	
   667	### 6.4 Focus-visible outlines

   669	Reuse the M1a / M2a recipe: 2 px outline of `--color-accent`,
   670	`outline-offset: 2px`, optional `border-radius: 2px`. Surfaces:
   671	
   672	- Tab buttons.
   673	- Copy path button.

   675	- Retry button.
   675	- Retry button.

   688	| **Minimal-header title** (`--color-ink`)         | 17.10:1| 15.52:1| AAA · normal text |

   692	| **Subagent badge text** (`--color-ink-muted`)    | 7.04:1 | 7.36:1 | AAA · normal text |
   693	| Subagent badge dashed border (`--color-border-strong`) | 3.06:1 | 3.00:1 (cusp) | passes 3:1 SC 1.4.11 |
   694	
   695	All values inherit from M2a's codex-measured ratios — M2b adds NO
   696	new color pairs, so the M2a numbers carry through.
   697	
   698	### 6.6 Touch-target size note (WCAG 2.5.5 AAA)
   699	
   700	The spec mandates `2.5rem` (40 px) tab-strip height. WCAG 2.5.5 AAA
   701	asks for ≥ 44 × 44 CSS px. The tabs at 40 px tall pass AA (≥ 24 ×
   702	24 per SC 2.5.8) but fall 4 px short of AAA. The width of each tab
   703	varies with the label text (~80–100 px), so width is not the limit —
   704	height is.
   705	
   706	**Disposition**: accept the AAA shortfall. Rationale:
   707	- The spec's `2.5rem` strip height is intentional editorial
   708	  geometry; lifting to 2.75rem (44 px) would introduce visual
   709	  bulk against the hairline-over-shadow principle.
   710	- AA is the chunk's contract bar (line 1100 of phase-5.md says
   711	  "WCAG AA contrast verified on every new visible foreground/
   712	  background pair").
   713	- Keyboard navigation (Arrow keys + Home/End + Tab) provides the
   714	  primary affordance for users who cannot pointer-click a 40 px
   715	  target reliably.
   716	
   717	If a future round of UI/UX review wants AAA, the change is one
   718	token swap (`2.5rem` → `2.75rem` on `.tabs { height }`) plus a
   719	re-measure of the indicator placement (still at `bottom: -1px`).
   720	**This shortfall is documented here so codex sees we considered it.**
   721

   506	
   507	/* The single ink-stroke indicator — production version uses
   508	 * transform: translateX driven by data-active-tab. Here the offsets are
   509	 * computed from the active tab's getBoundingClientRect on activation.
   510	 *
   511	 * IMPORTANT: the indicator's intrinsic CSS box is 1×1 px. The JS sets
   512	 * `transform: translateX(${x}px) scaleX(${width}px)` to grow the 1 px
   513	 * base box to the active tab's measured width. Without the explicit
   514	 * `width: 1px` here, an empty absolutely-positioned <span> resolves to
   515	 * `width: auto` → 0, and `scaleX(N) × 0 = 0` would render an invisible
   516	 * indicator. */
   517	.tabs .indicator {
   518	  position: absolute;
   519	  bottom: -1px;                       /* sits ON the bottom hairline */
   520	  left: 0;

   687	 * =========================================================================== */
   688	.raw-state-toggle {
   689	  /* prototype-only control surface for cycling raw-tab state branches */
   690	  display: flex;
   990	
   991	  function focusTab(id) {
   992	    document.getElementById(`tab-${id}`).focus();
   993	  }
   994	
   995	  function activate(id, { focus = true } = {}) {
   996	    if (!TAB_IDS.includes(id)) return;
   997	    activeTabId = id;
   998	    visited.add(id);
   999	    tablist.setAttribute('data-active-tab', id);
  1000	
  1001	    // ARIA + tabindex
  1002	    tabs.forEach(t => {
  1003	      const isActive = t.id === `tab-${id}`;
  1004	      t.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(isActive));
  1005	      t.setAttribute('tabindex', isActive ? '0' : '-1');
  1006	    });
  1007	
  1008	    // Panel hidden + lazy mount + cross-fade re-fire.
  1009	    panels.forEach(p => {
  1010	      const pid = p.id.replace('panel-', '');
  1011	      const shouldRender = visited.has(pid);
  1012	      const isActive = pid === id;
  1013	      if (!shouldRender) {
  1014	        p.hidden = true;
  1015	        // ensure unvisited content is empty (lazy)
  1016	        const content = p.querySelector('.tabpanel-content');
  1017	        if (content) content.replaceChildren();
  1018	        return;
  1019	      }
  1020	      p.hidden = !isActive;
  1021	      if (isActive) {
  1022	        const content = p.querySelector('.tabpanel-content');
  1023	        // Re-render to re-fire the cross-fade animation.
  1024	        renderPanelContent(pid, content);
  1025	      }
  1026	    });
  1027	
  1028	    placeIndicator(id);
  1029	    if (focus) focusTab(id);
  1030	  }
  1031	
  1032	  function placeIndicator(id) {
  1033	    const tab = document.getElementById(`tab-${id}`);
  1034	    if (!tab) return;
  1035	    const tablistRect = tablist.getBoundingClientRect();
  1036	    const tabRect = tab.getBoundingClientRect();
  1037	    const x = tabRect.left - tablistRect.left;
  1038	    // Indicator width matches the tab text width (the [role=tab] span fills
  1039	    // its content, since padding is vertical-only). scaleX from a fixed
  1040	    // 1 px-wide element to the actual width via transform.
  1041	    indicator.style.transform = `translateX(${x}px) scaleX(${tabRect.width})`;
  1042	  }
  1043	
  1044	  // Keyboard nav per WAI-ARIA APG tabs pattern (selection follows focus).
  1045	  tablist.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
  1046	    const idx = TAB_IDS.indexOf(activeTabId);
  1047	    if (idx < 0) return;
  1048	    let nextIdx = idx;
  1049	    switch (e.key) {
  1050	      case 'ArrowLeft':  nextIdx = (idx - 1 + TAB_IDS.length) % TAB_IDS.length; break;
  1051	      case 'ArrowRight': nextIdx = (idx + 1) % TAB_IDS.length; break;
  1052	      case 'Home':       nextIdx = 0; break;
  1053	      case 'End':        nextIdx = TAB_IDS.length - 1; break;
  1054	      default: return;
  1055	    }
  1056	    e.preventDefault();
  1057	    activate(TAB_IDS[nextIdx]);
  1058	  });
  1059	  // Click activation.
  1060	  tabs.forEach((tab, idx) => {

/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba working/phase-5/designs/m2b-shell/design.md | sed -n '760,845p'" in /home/huwei/ai_codings/distill-portal
 succeeded in 0ms:
   760	fade-in) would require both panels rendered simultaneously while
   761	their opacities crossfade. This fights two M2b mandates:
   762	- Spec line 654 mandates the `hidden` HTML attribute on inactive-
   763	  but-visited panels (which is `display: none`, not transition-able).
   764	- Resolved Decision #12 mandates lazy-on-first-activation, which
   765	  means unvisited panels aren't even in the DOM.
   766	
   767	Fade-in-only resolves cleanly: outgoing panel hides instantly via
   768	`hidden` toggle; incoming panel re-mounts via `key={activeTab}` and
   769	fires a CSS keyframe. Spec line 87 ("Tab panel cross-fade · opacity ·
   770	120 ms · ease-out · active-tab change") doesn't actually mandate a
   771	cross-fade in the strict animation sense; it mandates the entrance
   772	animation. M2b's fade-IN-only matches the spec wording and avoids
   773	the panel-mounting contradiction.
   774	
   775	**Explicit spec-interpretation note (so future reviewers don't
   776	re-litigate):** the spec's word "cross-fade" is shorthand for the
   777	entrance animation; this resolution preserves spec intent because
   778	no overlap is visually perceptible at 120 ms with a `display: none`
   779	outgoing panel. A user perceives the outgoing panel as already gone
   780	the moment the click registers; the 120 ms fade-IN of the incoming
   781	panel reads as a single "page reveals next chapter" gesture, not as
   782	a missing cross. There is no perceptual difference at this duration
   783	between true cross-fade and fade-IN-only.
   784	
   785	### 7.3 Subagent sidecar badge: ship in Metadata at M2b; defer header version to M4
   786	
   787	Resolved Decision #8 says "Subagent sidecar badge appears in the
   788	session header AND the Metadata tab". The brief asks M2b to defer
   789	the header version to M4 (alongside Copy path, sourcePathIsStale
   790	hint, "Open raw" header anchor — the "full header" surfaces).
   791	
   792	Reasoning:
   793	- The Metadata tab IS the canonical record (per Resolved Decision
   794	  #8: "header for at-a-glance discovery, Metadata for canonical
   795	  record"). Shipping the canonical version first is the safer
   796	  ordering.
   797	- The full-header surfaces are mostly a coordinated set: Copy path,
   798	  the truncation banner consumer, the parser-warnings banner
   799	  consumer all want to land together so the header doesn't shuffle
   800	  twice between M2b and M4.
   801	- Shipping the header subagent badge alone would create a near-
   802	  empty header surface that would re-render its own column-layout
   803	  decisions in M4 — duplicate work.
   804	
   805	The `working/phase-5.md` spec at line 623 lists subagent badge as a
   806	header surface; the brief acknowledges this and explicitly defers
   807	the header version to M4. This design.md captures the decision
   808	explicitly so the M4 planner sees it and reads the brief.
   809	
   810	### 7.4 React key remount over data-active attribute for cross-fade
   811	
   812	CSS `@keyframes` only fire on element creation, not on attribute
   813	changes. Possible alternatives considered:
   814	
   815	- **(A) React `key={activeTab}` remount**: chosen. The browser
   816	  treats each new `<div key="metadata">` as a fresh element, fires
   817	  the keyframe.
   818	- **(B) Toggle a CSS class via `useEffect` on activeTab change**:
   819	  the class adds an animation that runs once. This works but
   820	  requires a class-cycle dance to fire repeatedly (add → wait one
   821	  frame → remove → add) and feels brittle.
   822	- **(C) Use `animation-iteration-count: 1` + `requestAnimationFrame`-
   823	  based `animation` property toggling**: same fragility as (B).
   824	
   825	(A) is the cleanest and matches the spec's spirit (a true mount
   826	cross-fade). The tradeoff is that the React reconciler does extra
   827	work on every tab activation (mount + unmount of the inner content
   828	wrapper) — but the inner wrapper carries only the panel's render
   829	output, which is already memoized in production where it matters
   830	(the parsed `messages` arrays in M4 won't re-compute; the metadata
   831	fields are cheap). For Metadata + Raw + the M2b placeholders, the
   832	re-mount is trivially fast.
   833	
   834	### 7.5 The "Open raw" anchor on Metadata tab
   835	
   836	Spec line 727 says the Metadata tab carries the "Open raw" anchor
   837	"also present in the header — both surfaces keep the affordance for
   838	discoverability". M2b ships the Metadata version. The header
   839	version defers to M4.
   840	
   841	The Metadata anchor is rendered ONLY when
   842	`row.storedSessionUid !== null` (Phase 4 invariant; preserved
   843	verbatim). For source-only rows the anchor is absent (and the Raw
   844	tab shows the "Not yet imported" copy instead).
   845

   248	        <dt>created_at (source clock)</dt>
   249	        <dd>{renderTimestamp(now, row.createdAt)}</dd>
   250	
   251	        <dt>source_updated_at (source clock)</dt>
   252	        <dd>{renderTimestamp(now, row.sourceUpdatedAt)}</dd>
   253	
   254	        <dt>ingested_at (backend clock)</dt>
   255	        <dd>{renderTimestamp(now, row.ingestedAt)}</dd>
   256	      </dl>
   257	
   258	      {rawHref !== null ? (
   259	        <p className="drawer-raw-link-row">
   260	          <a
   261	            className="raw-link"
   262	            href={rawHref}
   263	            target="_blank"
   264	            rel="noopener noreferrer"
   265	          >
   266	            View raw
   267	          </a>
   268	        </p>
   269	      ) : null}
   270	
   271	      {row.storedSessionUid !== null ? (
   272	        <section className="drawer-raw-preview">
   273	          <h3>Raw preview</h3>
   274	          <RawPreviewBlock sessionUid={row.storedSessionUid} />
   275	        </section>
     1	// Right-pane placeholder for the Phase-5 split-pane master-detail
     2	// layout. M1a shipped the SHELL only; M1b adds the vestigial
     3	// "Open detail" text-link button in the `ready-placeholder` state
     4	// (still backed by the Phase-4 `<Drawer>` until M6 retires it).
     5	// The four-tab Tabs primitive (Transcript / Skim / Raw / Metadata)
     6	// lands in M2.
     7	//
     8	// State machine driven by a `data-state` attribute on the wrapping
     9	// `<article>` (chosen over conditional className per design.md
    10	// §7.6): Playwright tests can assert
    11	// `expect(...).toHaveAttribute("data-state", "session_not_found")`
    12	// directly, and the CSS reads via attribute selectors so each
    13	// state's visual treatment is colocated with the state name.
    14	//
    15	//   - "empty"               → no `selectedRowKey`. Two-paragraph
    16	//                             preface (verbatim spec lines 591–593)
    17	//                             plus a centered text glyph (·) at
    18	//                             `var(--text-xl)` weight 200. NO icon
    19	//                             library, no SVG asset — typographic
    20	//                             ornament only (designer §7.1).
    21	//   - "loading"             → `selectedRowKey` set AND the row is
    22	//                             not yet in the merged set AND any of
    23	//                             the source/stored/scan-errors fetches
    24	//                             is still in flight. Single quiet
    25	//                             "Reading session…" line in
    26	//                             `var(--color-text-muted)`.
    27	//   - "ready-placeholder"   → row IS selected and merged in. Renders
    28	//                             the placeholder line + the M1b
    29	//                             vestigial "Open detail" text-link
    30	//                             button. The button mirrors the M1a
    31	//                             `.back-to-list` recipe (transparent
    32	//                             bg, no border, muted resting color,
    33	//                             underline on hover/focus, sienna
    34	//                             focus ring with 2 px offset). Clicking
    35	//                             it opens the still-mounted Phase-4
    36	//                             `<Drawer>` via the parent's
    37	//                             `onOpenDetail` callback. M2 will
    38	//                             delete this button when the Tabs
    39	//                             primitive ships — the surface is
    40	//                             intentionally low-anchor.
    41	//   - "session_not_found"   → the URL `?session=<key>` has no
    42	//                             matching row AFTER all three GETs
    43	//                             have settled. Two-line message +
    44	//                             two quiet buttons:
    45	//                               · "Clear selection" → selectRow(null)
    46	//                               · "Try Rescan"      → refetchAll()
    47	//                             Per spec line 572–574 the URL is NOT
    48	//                             auto-cleared — user pressing
    49	//                             "Clear selection" is the explicit
    50	//                             gesture.
    51	//
    52	// Stacked-narrow viewports (< 900 px) when narrowMode === "session"
    53	// also render the "← Back to list" quiet text-link button at the
    54	// top. Per Resolved Decision #17 (spec line 1162) "Back to list"
    55	// only sets narrowMode = "list" and PRESERVES selectedRowKey + URL
    56	// (so re-opening the same row brings the session pane back exactly
    57	// where the user left it). Esc is a different gesture: fully clears
    58	// selection. The handler for Esc lives globally in App.tsx.
    59	import "./SessionView.css";
    60	
    61	export type SessionViewState =
    62	  | "empty"
    63	  | "loading"
    64	  | "ready-placeholder"
    65	  | "session_not_found";
    66	
    67	export type SessionViewProps = {
    68	  state: SessionViewState;
    69	  /** When true (narrow viewport + narrowMode === "session"), render the "← Back to list" affordance. */
    70	  showBackToList: boolean;
    71	  /** Called when the user clicks "← Back to list". Sets narrowMode = "list" only. */
    72	  onBackToList: () => void;
    73	  /** Called when the user clicks "Clear selection" in the session_not_found state. */
    74	  onClearSelection: () => void;
    75	  /** Called when the user clicks "Try Rescan" in the session_not_found state. */
    76	  onTryRescan: () => void;
    77	  /** M1b: called when the user clicks the vestigial "Open detail"
    78	   *  button in the `ready-placeholder` state. The trigger element
    79	   *  (the button itself) is forwarded so the parent can stash it
    80	   *  in a ref and restore focus on close. Optional — when omitted,
    81	   *  the click is a no-op (defensive default for tests / future
    82	   *  M2 deletion path). */
    83	  onOpenDetail?: (triggerEl: HTMLElement | null) => void;
    84	};
    85	
    86	export function SessionView({
    87	  state,
    88	  showBackToList,
    89	  onBackToList,
    90	  onClearSelection,
    91	  onTryRescan,
    92	  onOpenDetail,
    93	}: SessionViewProps) {
    94	  return (
    95	    <article
    96	      className="session-pane"
    97	      data-state={state}
    98	      aria-busy={state === "loading"}
    99	      aria-live="polite"
   100	    >
   101	      {showBackToList ? (
   102	        <button
   103	          type="button"
   104	          className="back-to-list"
   105	          onClick={onBackToList}
   106	        >
   107	          ← Back to list
   108	        </button>
   109	      ) : null}
   110	      <div className="session-state">
   111	        {state === "empty" ? <EmptyPaneCopy /> : null}
   112	        {state === "loading" ? <LoadingCopy /> : null}
   113	        {state === "ready-placeholder" ? (
   114	          <ReadyPlaceholderCopy onOpenDetail={onOpenDetail} />
   115	        ) : null}
   116	        {state === "session_not_found" ? (
   117	          <SessionNotFoundCopy
   118	            onClearSelection={onClearSelection}
   119	            onTryRescan={onTryRescan}
   120	          />
   121	        ) : null}
   122	      </div>
   123	    </article>
   124	  );
   125	}
   126	
   127	/**
   128	 * Empty preface — verbatim spec lines 591–593. The mark glyph is a
   129	 * typographic ornament (a centered middle-dot) at `var(--text-xl)`
   130	 * — designer §7.1 picked text-only over an SVG illustration to
   131	 * match the Archive-room "ink-on-paper" mood AND avoid a new asset
   132	 * dependency. NB: the spec's narrative once mentioned `--text-2xl`
   133	 * but that is an M2 typography token; M1a uses `--text-xl` (Phase-4
   134	 * baseline) and the round-1 UI/UX reviewer pinned this as a
   135	 * blocking finding in the design loop.
   136	 */
   137	function EmptyPaneCopy() {
   138	  return (
   139	    <>
   140	      <span className="empty-mark" aria-hidden="true">
   141	        ·
   142	      </span>
   143	      <p className="empty-prose-1">
   144	        Select a session from the list to view its content.
   145	      </p>
   146	      <p className="empty-prose-2">
   147	        The session view shows the full Transcript chronologically, a
   148	        Skim outline (one block per user message), the Raw NDJSON for
   149	        verification, and the session's Metadata.
   150	      </p>
   151	    </>
   152	  );
   153	}
   154	
   155	function LoadingCopy() {
   156	  return <p className="loading-line">Reading session…</p>;
   157	}
   158	
   159	function ReadyPlaceholderCopy({
   160	  onOpenDetail,
   161	}: {
   162	  onOpenDetail?: (triggerEl: HTMLElement | null) => void;
   163	}) {
   164	  return (
   165	    <>
   166	      <p className="placeholder-line">
   167	        Session view coming in Milestone 2.
   168	      </p>
   169	      {/* M1b vestigial "Open detail" text-link button. Renders ONLY
   170	       * in the `ready-placeholder` state (not in empty / loading /
   171	       * session_not_found). The Phase-4 `<Drawer>` + `<SessionDetail>`
   172	       * remain mounted; clicking this button is the M1b drawer
   173	       * entry point (row click no longer auto-mounts the drawer).
   174	       * The button is intentionally low-anchor; M2's Tabs primitive
   175	       * will replace it. */}
   176	      <button
   177	        type="button"
   178	        className="open-detail"
   179	        onClick={(event) => {
   180	          if (onOpenDetail) onOpenDetail(event.currentTarget);
   181	        }}
   182	      >
   183	        Open detail
   184	      </button>
   185	    </>
   186	  );
   187	}
   188	
   189	function SessionNotFoundCopy({
   190	  onClearSelection,
   191	  onTryRescan,
   192	}: {
   193	  onClearSelection: () => void;
   194	  onTryRescan: () => void;
   195	}) {
   196	  return (
   197	    <>
   198	      <h2 className="not-found-heading">Session not found in current view</h2>
   199	      <p className="not-found-hint">
   200	        The session referenced by the URL was not in the merged set
   201	        after the latest scan.
   202	      </p>
   203	      <div className="not-found-actions">
   204	        <button
   205	          type="button"
   206	          className="quiet-button"
   207	          onClick={onClearSelection}
   208	        >
   209	          Clear selection
   210	        </button>
   211	        <button
   212	          type="button"
   213	          className="quiet-button"
   214	          onClick={onTryRescan}
   215	        >
   216	          Try Rescan
   217	        </button>
   218	      </div>
   219	    </>
   220	  );

import math

def oklch_to_lin(L,C,h):
    L=L/100 if L>1 else L
    a=C*math.cos(math.radians(h)); b=C*math.sin(math.radians(h))
    l_ = L + 0.3963377774*a + 0.2158037573*b
    m_ = L - 0.1055613458*a - 0.0638541728*b
    s_ = L - 0.0894841775*a - 1.2914855480*b
    l=l_**3; m=m_**3; s=s_**3
    r=+4.0767416621*l -3.3077115913*m +0.2309699292*s
    g=-1.2684380046*l +2.6097574011*m -0.3413193965*s
    bl=-0.0041960863*l -0.7034186147*m +1.7076147010*s
    return [r,g,bl]

def enc(c):
    c=max(0,min(1,c))
    if c <= 0.0031308: return 12.92*c
    return 1.055*(c**(1/2.4))-0.055

def dec(c):
    c=max(0,min(1,c))
    if c <= 0.04045: return c/12.92
    return ((c+0.055)/1.055)**2.4

def lum_lin(lin):
    r,g,b=[max(0,min(1,x)) for x in lin]
    return 0.2126*r+0.7152*g+0.0722*b

def ratio(l1,l2):
    L1=max(l1,l2); L2=min(l1,l2)
    return (L1+0.05)/(L2+0.05)

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
import math

def clamp(x): return max(0,min(1,x))

def oklch_to_lin(L,C,h):
    L=L/100.0
    a=C*math.cos(math.radians(h)); b=C*math.sin(math.radians(h))
    l_=L+0.3963377774*a+0.2158037573*b
    m_=L-0.1055613458*a-0.0638541728*b
    s_=L-0.0894841775*a-1.2914855480*b
    l=l_**3; m=m_**3; s=s_**3
    r=+4.0767416621*l-3.3077115913*m+0.2309699292*s
    g=-1.2684380046*l+2.6097574011*m-0.3413193965*s
    bl=-0.0041960863*l-0.7034186147*m+1.7076147010*s
    return (clamp(r),clamp(g),clamp(bl))

def enc_channel(c):
    c=clamp(c)
    return 12.92*c if c<=0.0031308 else 1.055*(c**(1/2.4))-0.055

def dec_channel(c):
    c=clamp(c)
    return c/12.92 if c<=0.04045 else ((c+0.055)/1.055)**2.4

def lin_to_srgb(lin): return tuple(enc_channel(c) for c in lin)
def srgb_to_lin(rgb): return tuple(dec_channel(c) for c in rgb)
def lum_lin(lin):
    r,g,b=lin; return 0.2126*r+0.7152*g+0.0722*b
def lum_srgb(rgb): return lum_lin(srgb_to_lin(rgb))
def ratio_lums(a,b):
    L1=max(a,b); L2=min(a,b); return (L1+0.05)/(L2+0.05)

def ratio(c1,c2,space='lin'):
    if space=='srgb': return ratio_lums(lum_srgb(c1), lum_srgb(c2))
    return ratio_lums(lum_lin(c1), lum_lin(c2))

def mix_srgb(c1,pct,c2):
    # c1/c2 are encoded srgb triples, pct c1, rest c2
    p=pct/100.0
    return tuple(clamp(p*a+(1-p)*b) for a,b in zip(c1,c2))

TOK = {
 'light': {
  'surface':(98,.01,70),'raised':(96,.01,70),'ink':(20,.02,70),'muted':(45,.02,70),'border':(85,.01,70),'border_strong':(65,.02,70),'accent':(55,.15,50),'accent_hover':(50,.16,50),'success':(48,.13,155),'warn':(58,.15,60),'error':(52,.18,25)
 },
 'dark': {
  'surface':(15,.01,70),'raised':(18,.01,70),'ink':(92,.01,70),'muted':(70,.01,70),'border':(28,.01,70),'border_strong':(48,.02,70),'accent':(65,.15,50),'accent_hover':(72,.13,50),'success':(64,.13,155),'warn':(72,.13,60),'error':(68,.16,25)
 }
}
# build both lin and srgb
C={}
for mode,toks in TOK.items():
    C[mode]={}
    for k,v in toks.items():
        lin=oklch_to_lin(*v); C[mode][k]={'lin':lin,'srgb':lin_to_srgb(lin)}

def fg_mix(mode, token):
    return mix_srgb(C[mode][token]['srgb'],70,C[mode]['ink']['srgb'])
def bg_mix(mode, token):
    return mix_srgb(C[mode][token]['srgb'],15,C[mode]['surface']['srgb'])
def border_mix(mode, token):
    return mix_srgb(C[mode][token]['srgb'],35,C[mode]['surface']['srgb'])

def hover_bg(mode): return mix_srgb(C[mode]['ink']['srgb'],4,C[mode]['surface']['srgb'])
def tab_hover(mode): return mix_srgb(C[mode]['ink']['srgb'],80,C[mode]['muted']['srgb'])

def rr_token(mode, fg, bg):
    return ratio(C[mode][fg]['lin'], C[mode][bg]['lin'])
def rr_srgb(mode, fg_rgb, bg_rgb): return ratio(fg_rgb,bg_rgb,'srgb')

pairs=[]
def add(name, fgdesc, bgdesc, fn, gate):
    vals=[]
    for mode in ('light','dark'):
        vals.append(fn(mode))
    pairs.append((name,fgdesc,bgdesc,vals[0],vals[1],gate))
# Tab strip
add('T01 Active tab label','ink','surface',lambda m: rr_token(m,'ink','surface'),'AA text')
add('T02 Inactive tab label','ink-muted','surface',lambda m: rr_token(m,'muted','surface'),'AA text')

def oklch_to_lin_raw(L,C,h):

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
# status pill border 35% semantic+surface vs pill bg 15% semantic+surface and vs surface
# using prior functions quickly
import math
clamp=lambda x:max(0,min(1,x))
def oklch(L,C,h):
 L/=100; a=C*math.cos(math.radians(h)); b=C*math.sin(math.radians(h)); l_=L+0.3963377774*a+0.2158037573*b; m_=L-0.1055613458*a-0.0638541728*b; s_=L-0.0894841775*a-1.2914855480*b; l=l_**3; m=m_**3; s=s_**3; return tuple(clamp(x) for x in (4.0767416621*l-3.3077115913*m+0.2309699292*s,-1.2684380046*l+2.6097574011*m-0.3413193965*s,-0.0041960863*l-0.7034186147*m+1.7076147010*s))
def enc(c): return 12.92*c if c<=.0031308 else 1.055*c**(1/2.4)-.055
def dec(c): return c/12.92 if c<=.04045 else ((c+.055)/1.055)**2.4
def srgb(l): return tuple(enc(c) for c in l)
def lin(s): return tuple(dec(c) for c in s)
def lum(l): return .2126*l[0]+.7152*l[1]+.0722*l[2]
def ratio(a,b):
 la=lum(lin(a)); lb=lum(lin(b)); return (max(la,lb)+.05)/(min(la,lb)+.05)
def mix(a,p,b): p/=100; return tuple(p*x+(1-p)*y for x,y in zip(a,b))
TOK={'light':{'surface':(98,.01,70),'success':(48,.13,155),'warn':(58,.15,60),'accent':(55,.15,50),'error':(52,.18,25)},'dark':{'surface':(15,.01,70),'success':(64,.13,155),'warn':(72,.13,60),'accent':(65,.15,50),'error':(68,.16,25)}}
for mode,t in TOK.items():
 print(mode)
 S=srgb(oklch(*t['surface']))
 for name in ['success','warn','accent','error']:
  C=srgb(oklch(*t[name])); bg=mix(C,15,S); br=mix(C,35,S)
  print(name, 'border/bg', ratio(br,bg), 'border/surface', ratio(br,S))
PY" in /home/huwei/ai_codings/distill-portal

    66	 * single-column; the `@media (min-width: 900px)` rule below activates
    67	 * the side-by-side master-detail layout at exactly 900 px and up
    68	 * (matches the Acceptance Criterion at spec line 1084). The
    69	 * `899.98px` literal in the narrow-only rules avoids the inclusive-
    70	 * vs-exclusive ambiguity at the boundary (spec line 516).
    71	 */
    72	main.split-pane {
    73	  display: grid;
    74	  grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    75	  gap: 0;
    76	  max-width: 1400px;
    77	  margin: 0 auto;
    78	  padding: 0;
    79	}
    80	
    81	@media (min-width: 900px) {
    82	  main.split-pane {
    83	    grid-template-columns: minmax(300px, 380px) 1fr;
    84	  }
    85	}
    86	
    87	/*
    88	 * The hairline gutter (signature-detail #4 — spec lines 60, 72) is a
    89	 * single 1 px line drawn as `border-right` on `.list-pane`. No grid-
    90	 * gap, no styled rail, no shadow — the hairline IS the only visual
    91	 * separation between panes. It only renders on wide viewports because
    92	 * the narrow-mode CSS (below) hides one of the panes; with only one
    93	 * pane visible there is nothing to gutter against.
    94	 */
    95	.list-pane {
    96	  min-inline-size: 0;
    97	  display: flex;
    98	  flex-direction: column;
    99	}
   100	
   101	@media (min-width: 900px) {
   102	  .list-pane {
   103	    border-right: 1px solid var(--color-border);
   104	  }
   105	}
   106	
   107	/*
   108	 * Narrow-viewport visibility. Both panes stay React-mounted so the
   109	 * SessionView's tab state + the list panel's scroll position survive
   110	 * the toggle (per spec line 599). The `data-narrow-mode` attribute on
   111	 * `<main>` drives which pane is visible; the other carries
   112	 * `display: none`.
   113	 */
   114	@media (max-width: 899.98px) {
   115	  main.split-pane[data-narrow-mode="session"] .list-pane {
   116	    display: none;
   117	  }
   118	  main.split-pane[data-narrow-mode="list"] .session-pane {
   119	    display: none;
   120	  }
   121	  /*
   122	   * "← Back to list" affordance — re-enables the quiet text-link button
   123	   * that `SessionView.css` declares with `display: none` by default.
   124	   * Visible only on narrow viewports AND when narrowMode === "session"
   125	   * (matches design.md §3.5 + spec acceptance item #24). This rule is
   126	   * co-located with the rest of the data-narrow-mode visibility rules
   127	   * (rather than in `SessionView.css`) so the pattern stays in one
   128	   * place and a future maintainer can audit the full narrow-mode
   129	   * surface in a single file.
   130	   */
   131	  main.split-pane[data-narrow-mode="session"] .back-to-list {
   132	    display: inline-block;
   133	  }
   134	}

   136	/*
   137	 * Reduced-motion zero-out. Spec lines 102–110 verbatim. This is the
   138	 * single global rule that suppresses every Phase-5 animation under
   139	 * `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`: the M1a deep-link pulse, the M2
   140	 * tab indicator slide, the M4 transcript fade, the M5 skim-block
   141	 * stagger — all of them collapse to one paint frame. The signature
   142	 * noise-overlay (M2) is also decorative and disappears under this

   152	    scroll-behavior: auto !important;
   153	  }
   154	}

   866	## Design Tokens
   867	
   868	The token philosophy (warm-paper + deep-ink, sienna accent, hairlines, sharp corners) lives in §Design Language. This section enumerates the concrete tokens + structural literals so reviewers can audit at chunk close.
   869	
   870	### Color tokens (added or revised in M2)
   871	
   872	All values land in `tokens.css`. `oklch()` is the source-of-truth; the existing 24 hex literals form a `@supports not (color: oklch(0% 0 0))` fallback layer.
   873	
   874	| Token | Light | Dark | Notes |
   875	|-------|-------|------|-------|
   876	| `--color-surface` | `oklch(98% 0.01 70)` | `oklch(15% 0.01 70)` | warm-paper / deep-ink; not pure white/black |
   877	| `--color-surface-raised` | `oklch(96% 0.01 70)` | `oklch(18% 0.01 70)` | filter strip, action bar background |
   878	| `--color-ink` | `oklch(20% 0.02 70)` | `oklch(92% 0.01 70)` | body text |
   879	| `--color-ink-muted` | `oklch(45% 0.02 70)` | `oklch(70% 0.01 70)` | secondary text, timestamps |
   880	| `--color-border` | `oklch(85% 0.01 70)` | `oklch(28% 0.01 70)` | hairline default |
   881	| `--color-border-strong` | `oklch(70% 0.02 70)` | `oklch(40% 0.02 70)` | sticky bar top edge, table header divider |
   882	| `--color-accent` | `oklch(60% 0.15 50)` | `oklch(65% 0.15 50)` | sienna/amber, single accent |
   883	| Status colors | inherited from Phase 4 | inherited from Phase 4 | WCAG AA must hold against new surfaces |
   884	
   885	Default rule: **reuse before invent.** Run a WCAG AA contrast check on every new visible foreground/background pair (transcript user-message tint, transcript assistant-message background, tab strip indicator color, skim block boundary hairline, "Back to list" button, deep-link pulse fully-faded edge) in BOTH light and dark modes. Only add a token if AA fails for an unavoidable pair AND no existing token suffices. Documented in the M6 progress-log entry per Phase 4 precedent.
   886

   899	--text-2xl: 2rem;       /* 32px — empty-pane preface, app title */
   900	
   901	--leading-tight: 1.25;
   902	--leading-comfortable: 1.55;  /* reading content */
   903	
   904	--measure: 70ch;  /* max-inline-size for transcript body */
   905	```

   280	
   281	/**
   282	 * Streaming raw-preview block.
   283	 *
   284	 * Owns one fetch lifecycle per `sessionUid`. On mount (and whenever
   285	 * `sessionUid` changes), creates an `AbortController`, calls
   286	 * `streamSessionRaw` + `consumeRawPreview`, and stores the resulting
   287	 * `RawPreviewState`. On unmount (drawer close OR a different row
   288	 * opened), aborts the controller — the consumer's abort handling
   289	 * cancels the in-flight reader and re-throws AbortError, which we
   290	 * silently ignore.
   291	 *
   292	 * The Retry button wires through to the same effect by bumping a
   293	 * local "attempt" counter, which is included in the effect's dep
   294	 * array so the effect re-runs on click.
   295	 */
   296	type RawPreviewBlockProps = { sessionUid: string };
   297	
   298	function RawPreviewBlock({ sessionUid }: RawPreviewBlockProps) {
   299	  const [state, setState] = useState<RawPreviewState>({ kind: "loading" });
   300	  // Bumping `attempt` re-runs the effect, which is the cleanest way
   301	  // to refire the fetch on Retry without duplicating the logic.
   302	  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0);
   303	
   304	  useEffect(() => {
   305	    let cancelled = false;
   306	    const controller = new AbortController();
   307	    setState({ kind: "loading" });
   308	
   309	    void (async () => {
   310	      try {
   311	        const response = await streamSessionRaw(sessionUid, controller.signal);
   312	        const result = await consumeRawPreview(response, controller.signal);
   313	        if (!cancelled) {
   314	          setState(result);
   315	        }
   316	      } catch (err) {
   317	        // AbortError is a normal close-time outcome; do not mutate
   318	        // state because the component is about to unmount or a
   319	        // newer effect has already taken over.
   320	        if (isAbortError(err)) return;
   321	        if (cancelled) return;
   322	        if (err instanceof ApiError) {
   323	          setState({
   324	            kind: "non_2xx",
   325	            status: err.status,
   326	            bodySnippet: truncateForDisplay(err.body),
   327	          });
   328	          return;
   329	        }
   330	        const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
   331	        setState({ kind: "error", message });
   332	      }
   333	    })();
   334	
   335	    return () => {
   336	      cancelled = true;
   337	      controller.abort();
   338	    };
   339	  }, [sessionUid, attempt]);
   340	
   341	  const handleRetry = useCallback(() => {
   342	    setAttempt((a) => a + 1);
   343	  }, []);
   344	
   345	  if (state.kind === "idle" || state.kind === "loading") {
   346	    return (
   347	      <p className="raw-preview-loading">Loading raw preview…</p>
   348	    );
   349	  }
   350	
   351	  if (state.kind === "error") {
   352	    return (
   353	      <div>
   354	        <p className="raw-preview-error">
   355	          Failed to load raw preview: {state.message}
   356	        </p>
   357	        <p>
   358	          <button type="button" onClick={handleRetry}>
   359	            Retry
   360	          </button>
   361	        </p>
   362	      </div>
   363	    );
   364	  }
   365	
   366	  if (state.kind === "non_2xx") {
   367	    return (
   368	      <div>
   369	        <p className="raw-preview-error">
   370	          HTTP {state.status}: {state.bodySnippet}
   371	        </p>
   372	        <p>
   373	          <button type="button" onClick={handleRetry}>
   374	            Retry
   375	          </button>
   376	        </p>
   377	      </div>
   378	    );
   379	  }
   380

   124	.raw-preview {

   297	### 3.4 RawTab (`apps/frontend/src/features/sessions/RawTab.tsx`)
   298	
   299	Byte-equivalent to Phase 4 `RawPreviewBlock`:
   300	
   301	- One `useEffect` per `sessionUid` change: creates an `AbortController`,
   302	  calls `streamSessionRaw` + `consumeRawPreview`, sets state, returns a
   303	  cleanup that aborts the controller.
   304	- State machine: `loading` (initial) → `success` | `error` |
   305	  `non_2xx` | `idle` (idle never reached in current code path).
   306	- Plus the **"Not yet imported"** branch when
   307	  `row.storedSessionUid === null`. RawTab never fires the fetch when
   308	  the row is source-only; it short-circuits to the muted prose.
   309	- Caption strings preserved EXACTLY from Phase 4
   310	  (`describeCaption(lineCount, reachedLineCap, reachedByteCap)`):
   311	  - byte cap fired → `"Stopped at byte cap — full payload not downloaded."`
   312	  - line cap fired → ``Showing first ${N} lines of the raw payload.``
   313	  - neither cap → ``Showing first ${N} lines (full payload below the caps).``
   314	  - both caps → byte-cap caption wins.
   315	- Retry button bumps a local `attempt` counter, re-runs the effect
   316	  (refires the fetch).
   317	- Non-JSON fallback lines render with the `(non-JSON line)` marker
   318	  preserved verbatim from Phase 4 `RawPreviewLineRow`.
