- **Verdict**: needs changes

- **Findings**:
  - **BLOCKING** `working/phase-5/designs/m2b-shell/colors.md:122` + `prototype.html:740-747`: Raw non-JSON marker uses `--color-warn` as visible `--text-xs` text on `--color-surface-raised`. Computed contrast is **3.97:1 light / 7.34:1 dark**; light fails WCAG AA normal text. This also contradicts the claimed Phase 4 byte-equivalence in `design.md:299-318`; current `SessionDetail.css:143-156` uses muted text for this marker. Conflicts with `working/phase-5.md:1094` and the M2a warn-as-text fix-up.
  - **BLOCKING** `design.md:543-551`, `motion.md:28-34`, `prototype.html:483-484,629,673`: M2b adds animated `color`, `border-color`, and extra button hover `background-color` transitions. The frozen motion budget says every animated surface must be listed and only allows `transform`, `opacity`, and listed `background-color` surfaces; see `working/phase-5.md:84-95` and `working/phase-5.md:1100`.
  - **BLOCKING** `design.md:974,1115` + `prototype.html:52-60`: artifact uses `--space-5`, `--space-10`, and `--space-12`, but frozen `tokens.css:96-102` only defines `--space-1/2/3/4/6/8`. This violates the artifact’s own “no new tokens” rule in `design.md:132-136`; production CSS using these vars would compute invalid padding.
  - **BLOCKING** `design.md:222-225,810-832,1047-1053`: `key={activeTab}` around panel content remounts content on tab switches. That breaks the frozen lazy-then-keep-mounted contract and state preservation in `working/phase-5.md:650-658`; it can also abort/refetch `RawTab` despite the keep-mounted intent.
  - **BLOCKING** `design.md:626-629,633-646`: active `tabpanel` lacks a `tabIndex=0` rule. WAI-ARIA APG Tabs says Tab from the tablist moves to the tabpanel unless the first meaningful panel content is focusable, and panels without focusable content should set `tabindex="0"`: https://www.w3.org/WAI/ARIA/apg/patterns/tabs/. M2b Skim/Transcript placeholders have no focusable children, so the design’s keyboard matrix is incomplete.

- **WCAG Numerical Audit**:
  Computed with OKLCH -> Oklab -> clipped linear sRGB -> relative luminance. `color-mix(in srgb, ...)` rows are mixed in sRGB before luminance.

  | Pair / use | Light | Dark | Gate |
  |---|---:|---:|---|
  | `--color-ink` / `--color-surface` active tab, title, dd, buttons | 17.10:1 | 15.52:1 | PASS AA text |
  | `--color-ink-muted` / `--color-surface` inactive tab, metadata labels, placeholders | 7.04:1 | 7.36:1 | PASS AA text |
  | 80% ink + 20% muted / surface, inactive tab hover | 14.94:1 | 13.57:1 | PASS AA text |
  | `--color-accent` / `--color-surface`, indicator, focus ring, Open raw | 4.84:1 | 5.78:1 | PASS AA text and SC 1.4.11 |
  | `--color-accent-hover` / `--color-surface`, Open raw hover | 6.00:1 | 7.62:1 | PASS AA text |
  | `--color-border` / `--color-surface`, decorative hairlines | 1.49:1 | 1.35:1 | not gated |
  | `--color-border` / `--color-surface-raised`, raw pre border | 1.41:1 | 1.29:1 | not gated |
  | `--color-border-strong` / `--color-surface`, subagent/retry border | 3.06:1 | 3.00:1 | PASS SC 1.4.11, dark cusp |
  | warn pill fg/bg, outdated/conflict | 5.43:1 | 7.74:1 | PASS AA text |
  | success pill fg/bg | 6.69:1 | 6.94:1 | PASS AA text |
  | accent pill fg/bg | 5.95:1 | 6.62:1 | PASS AA text |
  | error pill fg/bg | 6.60:1 | 6.91:1 | PASS AA text |
  | `--color-ink` / 4% ink hover bg, copy/retry hover | 15.79:1 | 14.56:1 | PASS AA text |
  | `--color-ink` / `--color-surface-raised`, raw pre | 16.13:1 | 14.84:1 | PASS AA text |
  | `--color-ink-muted` / `--color-surface-raised`, non-JSON fallback line | 6.64:1 | 7.03:1 | PASS AA text |
  | `--color-warn` / `--color-surface-raised`, non-JSON marker as designed | **3.97:1** | 7.34:1 | **FAIL light AA text** |
  | 70% error + ink / surface, raw error prose | 8.36:1 | 8.22:1 | PASS AA text |

- **Missing Evidence**: none

- **Required Changes**:
  - Revert the Raw non-JSON marker to the Phase 4 muted recipe (`--color-ink-muted` / `--color-text-muted`) and update `colors.md` R03. Do not use `--color-warn` for normal-size marker text.
  - Remove M2b animations for `color`, `border-color`, and unlisted button hover backgrounds from `design.md`, `motion.md`, and the prototype. Keep those hover state changes instant unless a coordinator waiver updates the frozen motion table.
  - Replace undefined spacing vars with existing tokens or documented structural literals. Do not add `--space-5/10/12` in M2b.
  - Replace the `key={activeTab}` content-remount fade with a stable mounted panel content tree plus a non-remounting animation restart mechanism.
  - Add `tabIndex={0}` to active tabpanels, or at minimum to panels whose first meaningful content is not focusable, and add keyboard tests for Tab from tab into panel.

- **Notes**:
  - Fade-in-only is a sound resolution for `hidden` plus opacity motion, but only if it does not remount the stateful panel content.
  - The 1 px indicator sizing fix is mathematically sound: `width:1px` with `scaleX(tabWidth)` produces a visible stroke. Add production acceptance text for resize/reflow remeasurement and either enforce `padding-inline:0` on tab buttons or measure an inner label span.
tokens used
278,371
- **Verdict**: needs changes

- **Findings**:
  - **BLOCKING** `working/phase-5/designs/m2b-shell/colors.md:122` + `prototype.html:740-747`: Raw non-JSON marker uses `--color-warn` as visible `--text-xs` text on `--color-surface-raised`. Computed contrast is **3.97:1 light / 7.34:1 dark**; light fails WCAG AA normal text. This also contradicts the claimed Phase 4 byte-equivalence in `design.md:299-318`; current `SessionDetail.css:143-156` uses muted text for this marker. Conflicts with `working/phase-5.md:1094` and the M2a warn-as-text fix-up.
  - **BLOCKING** `design.md:543-551`, `motion.md:28-34`, `prototype.html:483-484,629,673`: M2b adds animated `color`, `border-color`, and extra button hover `background-color` transitions. The frozen motion budget says every animated surface must be listed and only allows `transform`, `opacity`, and listed `background-color` surfaces; see `working/phase-5.md:84-95` and `working/phase-5.md:1100`.
  - **BLOCKING** `design.md:974,1115` + `prototype.html:52-60`: artifact uses `--space-5`, `--space-10`, and `--space-12`, but frozen `tokens.css:96-102` only defines `--space-1/2/3/4/6/8`. This violates the artifact’s own “no new tokens” rule in `design.md:132-136`; production CSS using these vars would compute invalid padding.
  - **BLOCKING** `design.md:222-225,810-832,1047-1053`: `key={activeTab}` around panel content remounts content on tab switches. That breaks the frozen lazy-then-keep-mounted contract and state preservation in `working/phase-5.md:650-658`; it can also abort/refetch `RawTab` despite the keep-mounted intent.
  - **BLOCKING** `design.md:626-629,633-646`: active `tabpanel` lacks a `tabIndex=0` rule. WAI-ARIA APG Tabs says Tab from the tablist moves to the tabpanel unless the first meaningful panel content is focusable, and panels without focusable content should set `tabindex="0"`: https://www.w3.org/WAI/ARIA/apg/patterns/tabs/.
